G.SJL 1069, — In pursuance of sub-paragraph (2) of Para- 
graph 3 of the Sixth Schedule to the Central Secretariat 
Stenographers Service Rules, 1969, tho Central Govern- 
ment in the Ministry of Home Affairs (Department of Per- 
sonnel and Administrative Reforms) in consultation with the 
Union Public Service Commission hereby makes the follow- 
ing regulations further to amend the Central Secretariat 
Stenographers Service Grade B (Limited Departmental 
Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1974, namely: — 

J. (1) These regulations may be called the Central 
Secretariat Stenographers Grade R (Limited Departmental 
Competitive Examination) (Amendment) Regulations, 1978. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. In regulation 4 of the Central Secretariat Stenographers* 
Service Grade B (Limited Departmental Competitive Exami- 
nation) Regulations, 1974, in the proviso to clause (t), for 
the words “Provided that,” the words “Provided further 
that” shall be substituted and before that proviso as so 
amended, the following proviso shall be inserted, namely : — 

“Provided that if ho had been appointed to the Grade 
‘O of the Central Secretariat Stenographers Service 
on the results of the competitive examination 
including a Limited Departmental Competitive 
Examination, such an examination should have been 
held not less than five years before the crucial date 
and he should have rendered not less than four years 
approved and continuous service in that grade”.

MINISTRY OF EXTERNAL AFFAIRS 
New Delhi, the 16th August, 1978 

G.S.R. 1072.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby make 
the following rules, regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Joint Secretary (Legal and Treaties Division) of the Ministry of 
External Affairs, namely :— 

1. Short Title and Commencement.— (1) These rules may be called the Joint Secretary (Legal and Treaties Division) of the Ministry 
of External Affairs Recruitment Rules, 1978, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, Classification and Scale of Pay,— The number of posts, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto shall be 
as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed hereto, 

3. Method of Recruitment, Age limit, Qualifications etc,. — The method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, qualifications and 
other matters connected therewith shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid: 

Provided that the maximum age limit specified in column 6 of the said Schedule may bo relaxed in the case of candidates belonging 
tp the Scheduled Castes and the Scheduled Tribes or other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time. 

4. Disqualification.— No person shall be eligible for appointment to the said post (a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage 
with a person having a spouse living or (b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, provided 
that the Central Govt, may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such person and the other 
party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to Relax. — Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by order, for 
reasons to be recorded in writing, and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions of these 
rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving.— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes 
and the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from 
time to time.

MINISTRY OF HEALTH AND FAMILY WELFARE 
(Department of Health) 

New Delhi, the 18th August, 1978 

G.S.R. 1073, — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the post of Publicity Officer (Audio Visual 
Aids) in the Central Health Education Bureau of the 
Directorate General, New Delhi, namely : — 

1, Short title and commencement. — Thecs rules may be 
called the Publicity Officer (Audio Visual Aids) (Group ‘B* 
Gazetted post) Recruitment Rules, 1978. 

They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2, Number, dass/fication and scale of pay. — The number 
of post, it’s classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto, shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said 
Schedule (annexed hereto). 

3, Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifica- 
tions. — The method of recruitment, age-limit, qualifica- 
tions and other matters relating to the said post shall be 
as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 


4. Disqualification, — No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage 

with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post ; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the 
marriage and that there are other grounds for so doing, 
exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

5 Power to relax. — Where the Central Government! is 
of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it 
may, by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, 
and in consultation with the Union Public Service Com- 
mission, relax any of the provisions of these rules with 
respect to any class or category of persons. 

Savings, — Nothing in these rules shall affect reserva- 
tions. relaxation of age limit, and other concessions 
required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, the 
Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons In 
accordance with the orders issued by the Central Govern- 
ment from time to time in this regard.

New Delhi, Ihe 1 9th August, 197R 

G.S.R. 1074.— Whereas ceitain draft rules* further to 
amend the Drugs and Cosmestics Rules, 1945. were 
published as required by Sections 12 and 33 of th: Drugs 
and Cosmeticj Act, 1940 t23 of 1940) at pages 263 to 
267 of the Gazette of India Parti II, Section 3, Sub-scction 


And whereas the objections and suggestions received 
from the public on the said draft rules have been con- 
sidered by the Central Government ; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
Sections 12 and 33 of the Drugs and Cosmetics Act, 1940 
the Central Government after consultation with the Drugs 
Technical Advisory Board, hereby makes the following 
rules further to amend the Drugs and Cosmetics Rules, 
1945, namely: — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Drugs and 
Cosmetics (Third Amendment) Rules, 1978 ; 

f2) They shall come into force on the date erf their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2 . In the Drugs; and Cosmetics Rules, 1945 (hereinafter 
referred to as the said rules), 

in rule 127 of the said rules, in sub-rule O ) — - 

(i) in item ( 1 ) relating to Natural Colours after 
the enliy "Yellow Oxide of Iron’, the follow- 
ing entry shall be inserted, namely 

‘Titanium Dioxide”. 

Cii) in item (3) relating to 'Coal Tar Colours’, 

fa) under the heading Green after the item 

‘Alizarin Cyanine Green F’ and the entiies 
relating thereto, the following shall be inser- 
ted, namely : — 

c Fasst Green 42053 Disodium salt of 4- { [4-(N-ethyl- 
FCF P-sulfob;nzhlrmino)-pheny]-]‘-4- 

hydroxy-7 -s ul fon iumpheny I )-methy-]^

(2-nphtV>F3 t 6 -disul phonic acid);’’ 

(b) under the beading ‘Blue* after the item 

Indigo Carmine* and the entries relating 
thereto, the following shall be inserted, 
namely : — 

“Brillium 42090 Disolium salt of 4-[ [4-(N-ethyl- 
Blu? FCF p-sulphob"“l 7 yl vnmoFphenyl-( 2 -

ing to “Alizurol purple SS” shall be omitted. 



1994 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : SEPTEMBER 2, I978/BHADRA 11, 1900 [Part II— Sec. 3(i)J 


3. After rule 135 of the said rules, the following rule 
shall be inserted, namely ; — 

“135A. Import of consmtlics containing mercury com- 
ponds prhibited. — No cosmetic shall be imported 
which contains mercury compounds.” 

4. After rule 145-B of the said rules, the following rule 
shall be inserted, namely : — 

"1450. Prohibition of manufacture of cosmetic con- 
taining mercury compounds. — No cosmetic con- 

training mercury compounds shall be manufactured.” 

5. In Schedule K of the said rules, against entry _ 5, 
under the heading “Extent and Conditions of Exemption'* 
for condition fl), the following condition shall be substitu- 
ted, namely 

"(1) The drugs shall be purchased only from a dealer 
or a manufacturer licensed under those rules, 
and records of such purchases showing the names 
and quantities of such drugs, together with their 
batch numbers and the names and addressed of 
the manufacturers shall be maintained. Such 
records shall be open to inspection by an Inspector 
appointed under the Act, who may, if necessary, 
make enquiries about purchases of the drugs and 
may also take samples for test.”

New Delhi, the 22nd August, 1978 

G.S.R. 1075. — The following draft of certain rules further 
to amend the Drugs and Cosmetics Rules, 1945 which the 
Central Government proposes to make, after consultation 
with the Drugs Technical Advisory Board, in exercise of 
the powers conferred by Sections 12 and 33 erf the Drugs and 
Cosmetics Act, 1940 (23 of 1940), is hereby published, as 
required by the said sections, for the information of all 
persons likely to be affected thereby; and notice is hereby 
given that ,tlhc said draft rules will be taken into consideration 
after the expiry of a period of 90 days from the date on 
which the copies of the Official Gazette in which this noti- 
fication is published are available to the public. 

Any objections 01 suggestions which may be received from 
any person with respect to the said draft rules before the 
expiry of the period so specified will be taken into consi- 
deration by the Central Government.

DRAFT RULES 

L These rules may be called the Drugs and Cosmetics 
(Amendment) Rules, 1978. 

2. In the Drugs and Cosmetics Rules, 1945 (hereinafter 
referred to as the said rules), in rules 65, — 

(a) in sub-rule (4), for clause (4), the following clause 
shall be substituted, namely ; — 

4, (4) (i) Records of purchase of a drug intended for 
sale or sold by retail shall be maintained by the 
licensee in a register specially maintained for the 
purpose in which the following particulars shall 
be entered, namely — 

(a) the date of purchase, 

(b) the name and address of the person from whom 
purchased and the number of the relevant 
licence held by him, 

(c) the name of the drug, the quantity and the batch 
number, and 



1996 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : SEPTEMBER 2, 1978/BHADRA II, 1900 [Part II— Src. 3(|)] 

(d) the name of the manufacturer of the drug. 


( ii) Purchase bilb including cash or credit memos shall 
be maintained by the licensee as records."; 

(b) in sub-rule (5), for clause (3), the following 
clause shall be substituted, namely : — 

“(3)(i) Records of purchase of a drug intend- 
ed for rc-sale or sold hy wholesale shall be 
maintained by the licenesce in a register spe- 
cially maintained for the purpose in which the 
following particulars shall be entered, 
namely ; — 

fa) the date of purchase, 

(b) the name and actress of the relevant licence hied 
by him, 

(c) the name of the manufacturer of the drug, 

(d) the name of the manufacturer of the drug. 

fii) Purchase bills including cash or credit memos 
shall be maintained by the licensee as records. 

3. After rule 66 of the said rules, the following rule shall 
be inserted, namely ; — 

“66. A. Procedure for disposal of drugs in the event 
of cancellation of licence. — 

fl) In case, a licensee, whose licence has been cancelled, 
desires to dispose of the drugs he has in his possession in the 
premises in respect of which the licence has been cancelled, 
he shall apply in writing to the licensing authority for this 
purpose, giving the following particulars — 

(a) the name and address of the person to whom the 
drugs are proposed to be sold or supplied together 
with the number of the licence for sale or manu- 
facture, as the case may he, held by him. 

(b) the names ol : drugs together with their quantities 

batch numbers, the names and addresses of their 
manufacturers an<i the dates of their expiry, if 
any, proposed to be sold to the person mentioned 
in clause (u), 

2. The licensing authority may after examination of the 
particulars referred to in sub-rule (1) and, if necessary, after 
inspection by an Inspector of the premises where the drugs 
are stocked, grant the necessary permission for their disposal". 

4. In rule 71 of the said rules, in condition 1, for the first 
proviso, the following provisos shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Provided that for the manufacture of formulations no 
person, other than the one specified in clause la), 
shall deemed to be qualified for the purpose of this 
rule. 

Explanation. — Formulation includes a drug which Is 
processed into a dosage form ready for internal or 


external administration to human beings or noi/nals. 
animals, 

Provided further that any person who was immediately 
before the commencement of the Drugs and Cos- 
metics (Amendment), Rules, 1978 actively direct- 
i;-fg or personally supervising the manufacture of 
drugs and whose name was accordingly entered 
in Form 25 as it existed before that date shall be 
deemed to be qualified for the purpose of this 
rule.". 

5, In rule 76 of the said rules, in condition 1, for the 
first proviso, the following provisos shall be substituted, 
namefy 

“Provided that for the manufacture of formulations no 
person, other than the one specified in caluse (a), 
shall be deemed to be qualified for the purpose of 
this rule. 

Explanation, — Formulation includes a drug which ii 

processed into a dosage form ready for internal or 
external administration to human beings or animals. 

Provided further that any person who was immediately 
before the commencement of the Drugs and Cos- 
metics (Amendment), Rules, 1978 actlivdy directing 
and personally supervising the manufacture of drugs 
and whose name was accordingly entered in Form 
28 as it existed before that date shall be deemed to 
be qualified for the purpose of this rule.". 

6, After rule 104 of the said rules, the following rule shall 
be inserted, namely ; — 

“104A. Prohibition against altering inscriptions on con- 
tainers labels or wrappers of drug. — 

No person shall after, obliterate or deface any inscription 
or mark made or recorded by the manufacture on the con- 
tainers, labels or wrappers of drug, — 

Provided that nothing in ( t{his rule shall apply to any 
alteration of any inscription or mark made on the 
container, label or wrapper of any drug at the 
instance or direction or with the permission of the 
licensing authority". 

7, Rule 1 10A of the said rules shall be omitted. 

8, In Schedule C to the said rules, after entry 14 the 
following entry shall be inserted, namely : — 

“J 5. Ophthalmic preparations;”.

MINISTRY OF STEEL AND MINES 
(Department of Mines) 

New Delhi, the 9th August, 1978 

G.S.R. 1076.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Indian Bureau 
of Mines (Class III Ministerial posts) Recruitment Rules, 
1965, namely : — 

(i) for serial No* 1 and entries relating thereto, the following

No. post posts

(Amendment) Rules, 1978. 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette.

III Ministrial posts) Recruitment Rules, 1965,. —

duled Tribes — Member 

4. Administrative Officer, 

In-charge of Establish- 
ment Section — Member. 

The crucial date for determining the age limit mentioned in column 7 (of the Recruitment Rules) will in each c; sc be the 
closing date for receipt of applications from candidates in India (other than Andaman and Nicobar Islands anf Lakshad- 
weep), In case of appointment to be made through the Employment Exchanges, the curcial date for detci i :ning the 
age limit will in each case be the last date upto which the Employment Exchanges arc asked to submit the names. 

The qualification regarding experience prescribed for direct recruits is relaxable in each cu^e ai the discretion rf the com- 
petent authority in case of candidates belonging to the Scheduled Castes or Scheduled Tribes, if at any stage of selection 
the competent authority is of the opinion that sufficient number of candidates belonging to these communities possessing 
the requisite experience arc not likely to be available to fill up the posts reserved for them.

G,S.R, 1077 -In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President herehy makes 

the following rules further to amend the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1965, namely : — 

1, (1) These rules may be called the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class IV posts) Recruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1978, 

(ii) They shall come in to force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1965 ; — 

(i) for serial No. 1 and entries relating thereto, the following serial number and entries shall be substituted, namely : —

Note 1 :— The crucial date for determining the age limit mentioned in column 7 will in each case be the closing date for receipt 
of applications from candidates in India (other than Andaman and Nicobar Islands and Lakshadweep). In case of 
appointment to be made through the Employment Exchanges, the crucial date for determining the age limit will in each 
case be the last date upto which the Employment Exchanges arc asked to submit the names. 


Note 2 The qualification regarding experience prescribed for direct recruits is relaxable in each case at the discretion of the com- 
petent authority in case of candidates belonging to the Scheduled Castes or Scheduled Tribes, if at any stage of selection 
the competent authority is of the opinion that sufficient number of candidates belonging to these communities possessing 
the requisite experience are not likely to be available to fill up the posts reserved for them.

MINISTRY OF STEEL AND MINES 

(Department of Mines) 

New Delhi, the 9th August, 1978 

0. 5.R. 1078. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to Article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class III Non-Ministerial Posts) Recruitments Rules, 1965 
namely: — 

1 . (1) These rules may be called the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class III Non-Ministerial Posts) Recmitment (Amendment) Rules, 

1978, 


(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in tho Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Indian Bureau of Mines (Class III Non-Ministerial Posts) Recruitment Rules, 1965, — 

(i) for serial No. 3 and entries relating thereto, the following serial number and entries shall be substituted, namely: —

2060 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : SEPTEMBER 2 , 1978/BHADRA 11, 1900 [Part II— Sec. 3(1)] 


MINISTRY OF AGRICULTURE & IRRIGATION 

(Department of Agriculture) 

New Delhi, the 17th August, 1978 

G.S.R, 108L^In exercise of the power conferred by the 
rovjso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the post of Senior Research Assistant at 
Pelagic Fisheries Project, namely : — 

1* Short title and commencement.- — (t) These rules may 
be called the Pelagic Fisheries Project (Senior Research 
Assistant) Recruitment Rules, 1978. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pay. — The number 
of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay 
attached thereto, shall be as specified in column 2 to 4 of 
the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

2, Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc. — 
The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications, and 
Other matters relating to he said post shall be as specified 
in column 5 to 13 of the said schedule. 


Name of post No. of Classification Scale of pay 
posts 


4. Disqualifications. — No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a mafnage 

with a person having a spouse living, oi 

(b) who, having a spouse living has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the 
marriage and that there are grounds for so doing, exempt 
any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is 
of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may* 
by order for reasons to be recorded in writing and in 
consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, 
relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any 
class or category of persons, 

6. Savings. — Nothing in these rules shall affect reserva- 
tions and other concessions required to be provided for the 
Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special 
categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued 
by the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF TOURISM & CIVIL AVIATION 
New Delhi, the 21st August, 1978 

G.S.R, 1083. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, and in super- 
session of the Representative of India on the Council of 
International Civil Aviation Organisation (Recruitment) 
Rules, 1970, the President hereby makes the following rules 
regulating the method of recruitment to the post of 
Representative of India on the Council of International 
Civil Aviation Organisation, Montreal (Canada), Civil 
Aviation Department, namely : — - 

1. Short title and commencement.-— (1 ) These rules may 
he called the Representative of India on the Council of 
International Civil Aviation Organisation (Recruitment) 
Rules, 1978. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pav. — The number 
of post, its classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the 
Schedule annexed to the rules. 


3. Method of Recruitment, age limit and other qualifica- 
tions.' — The method of recruitment to the said post, age 
limit and other matters connected Therewith shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualification. — -No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage 

with a spouse living, or 

(b) who having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, shall be 
eligible for appointment to the said post. 

5. Nothing in these rules shall affect the reservation, 
age relaxation or other concession required to be provided 
for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes, and other 
categories of persons in accordance with the rules issued 
from time to time in this regard. 

6. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is 
of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
after consultation with the Union Public Service Com- 
mission, by order, and for reasons to be recorded in writing, 
relax any of the previsions of these rules in respect of any 
class or category of persons.

MINISTRY OF INFORMATION AND BROADCASTING 
New Delhi, the 8th August, 1978 

G.S.R. 1084. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment! to the post of Administrative Officer (Group A) 
in the Central Board of Film Censors under the Ministry 
of Information and Broadcasting, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement. — (1) These rules may 
be called the Central Board of Film Censors (Administra- 
tive Officer, Group A) Recruitment Rules, 1978. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette, 

2. Number oi posts, classification and scale of pay. — -The 
number of post, its classification arid the scale of pay attach- 
ed thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the 
Schedule annexed lo these rules, 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifica- 
tions. — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said post shall be as speci- 
fied in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 

Provided that the upper age limit specified in column 6 
of the said Schedule may be relaxed in the case of the 
candidates belonging to the Scheduled Castes or the Sche- 
duled Tribes and other special categories of persons in ac- 
cordance^ with the general orders issued from time (o time 
by the Central Government in this regard. 


Probation. — F.very person appointed whether by direct 
recruitment or otherwise to the said post shall be on proba- 
tion for the period specified in column 9 of the said Sche- 
duled : 

Provided that! the period of probation may be extended 
by the appointing authority for such further period not ex- 
ceeding two years, on year to year basis, as it deem* fit. 

5. Di&qualifi cation.— No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a pci son having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, shall be 
eligible foi appointment to the said post ; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the Personal Law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marri- 
age and there are other grounds for so doing exempt any 
pei son from the operation of this rule. 

b. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, and in con- 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax 
any of the pi o visions of these rules with respect to any 
class or category of persons. 

7. Saving.- — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for the Sche- 
duled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and offier special cate- 
gories of persons in accoi dance with the orders issued by 
the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF LABOUR 

New Delhi, the 19 th August, 1978 

G.SLR 1085.— In exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 5, read with section 7, of the Coal Mines Provident 
Fund and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, F748 (46 of 1948), 
the Central Government hereby makes the following Scheme 
further to amend tihe Coal Mines Bonus Scheme namely n 

1, (1) This Scheme may be called the Coal Mines Bonus 
( Amendment) Scheme, 1978. 

(2) It shall come into force on the date of Its publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2, In sub-paragraph (2) of paragraph Jl of the Coal 
Mines Bonus Scheme for the words, “Provided that 1 ’ the 
following shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Provided that where any employer is obtaining the 
signature or left thumb impression of his employees 
in the acquittance roll for payment of bonus and 
the Chief Labour Commissioner (Central) is of 
the opinion that in such case the signature or left 
thumb impression of the employees required to be 
affixed in the Bonus Register maintained in Form 
X, need not be so affixedj he may, by order, permit 
such employer to maintain the register in Form X 
without obtaining the signature or left thumb im- 
pression of such employees. 

Provided further that

sub-section (1) of section 25 of the Customs Act, 1962 (52

necessary in the public interest so to do, hereby makes the 
following amendment in the notification of the Government 
of India in the Department of Revenue and Banking, No. 141- 
Customs, dated the 2nd August, 1976, namely : — 

in the specifications mentioned under the said notifica- 
tion, item (ii) and the entries relating thereto shall 
be omitted,

G*S.R. 1090. — In) exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1) of section 25 of the Customs Act, 1962 
(52 of 1962), the Central Government, being satisfied that 
it is necessary in the public interest so to do, hereby rescinds 
the notification of the Government of India, in the Ministry 
of Finance, Department of Revenue No. 62-Customs, dated 
the 9th March, 1978.

New Delhi, the 2nd September, 1978 
CENTRAL EXCISES 

G.S.R. 1091. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-rule (1) of rule 8 of the Central Excise Rules, 1944, the 
Central Government hereby makes the following further 
amendment in the notification of the Government of India in 
the Ministry of Finance (Department of Revenue and Insu- 
rance) No. 145/71-Central Excises, dated the 26th July, 
1971, namely : — 

In the tabFe appended to the said notification, against 
Serial No. 6, in column 4, for the words “total pro- 
duction”, the words “total production during a fiin- 
ancial year in which such consumption E made” 
shall be substituted.
